Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the symplectic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 51. The moduli space has dimension at most 466 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to symplectic structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 51/466 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on symplectic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 642. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 306 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to symplectic theory, we decompose the problem into 642 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 306. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
